!= NULL) {\n        memcpy(buf, node->data + fd_table[fd].position, bytes_to_read);\n        fd_table[fd].position += bytes_to_read;\n    }\n    \n    return bytes_to_read;\n}\n\n// SYS_write: Write // Little endian\n        this.pc += 4;\n        \n        // Decode instruction\n        const opcode = (instruction >>> 26) & 0x3F;\n        const rs = (instruction >>> 21) & 0x1F;\n        const rt = (instruction >>> 16) & 0x1F;\n        const rd = (instruction >>> 11) & 0x1F;\n        const shamt = (instruction >>> 6) & 0x1F;\n        const funct = instruction & 0x3F;\n        const immediate = instruction & 0xFFFF;\n        const address = instruction & 0x3FFFFFF;\n        \n        // Sign extend immediate\n        const simmediate = (immediate & 0x8000) ?        break;\n                \n            case 0x03: // jal (jump and link)\n                this.registers[31] = this.pc; // Save return address\n                this.pc = (this.pc & 0xF0000000) | (address << 2);\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x04: // beq (branch if equal)\n                if (this.registers[rs] === this.registers[rt]) {\n                    this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n                }\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x05: // bne (branch if not equal)\n                if (this.registers[rs] !== this.registers[rt]) {\n                    this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n                }\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x06: // blez (branch if <= 0)\n                if (this.registers[rs] <= 0) {\n                    this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n                }\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x07: // bgtz (branch if > 0)\n                if (this.registers[rs] > 0) {\n                    this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n                }\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x08: // addi\n \n            case 0x0B: // sltiu\n                this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] >>> 0 < simmediate >>> 0) ? 1 : 0;\n                break;\n                \n            case 0x0C: // true); // Little endian\n176:         this.pc += 4;\n177:         \n178:         // Decode instruction\n179:         const opcode = (instruction >>> 26) & 0x3F;\n180:         const rs = (instruction          this.executeRType(funct, rs, rt, rd, shamt);\n195:                 break;\n196:                 \n197:             case 0x02: // j (jump)\n198:                 this.pc = (this.pc & address\n203:                 this.pc = (this.pc & 0xF0000000) | (address << 2);\n204:                 break;\n205:                 \n206:             case 0x04: // beq (branch if equal)\n207:                 if (this.registers[rs] === this.registers[rt]) {\n208:                     this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n209:                 }\n210:                 break;\n211:                 \n212:             case 0x05: // bne (branch if not equal)\n213:                 if (this.registers[rs] !== this.registers[rt]) {\n214:                     this.pc += simmediate << 2;\n215:                 }\n216: << 2;\n221:                 }\n222:                 break;\n223:                 \n224:             case 0x07: // bgtz (branch if > 0)\n225:                 if (this.registers[rs] > 0) {\n226: | 0;\n236:                 break;\n237:                 \n238:             case 0x0A: // slti\n239:                 this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] < simmediate) ? 1 : 0;\n240: break;\n245:                 \n246:             case 0x0C: // andi\n247:                 this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] & immediate;\n248:                 break;\n249:                 \n250:             case 0x0D: // ori\n251:                 this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] | immediate;\n252:                 break;\n253:                 \n254:             case 0x0E: // xori\n255:            } else {\n308:                     console.warn(`Unknown opcode: 0x${opcode.toString(16)} at PC=0x${(this.pc-4).toString(16)}`);\n309:                 }\n310:                 break;\n311:         }\n312:         \n313:         // Register 0 (zero) is always 0\n314:         this.registers[0] = 0;\n315:     }\n316:     \n317:     // Execute R-type instructions\n318:     executeRType(funct, rs, rt, rd, shamt) {\n319:         switch (funct) {\n320:             case 0x00: // sll (shift left logical)\n321:                 this.registers[rd] = this.registers[rt] << shamt;\n322: // Success\n        } else {\n            this.registers[2] = -1; // Error\n        }\n    }\n    \n    sys_lseek() {\n        const fd = this.registers[4]; // a0\n        const offset = this.registers[5]; // a1\n        const whence = this.registers[6]; // a2\n        \n        if (this.fileDescriptors[fd]) {\n            try {\n                const fileInfo = this.fileDescriptors[fd];\n                const stats = fs.statSync(fileInfo.path);\n                const fileSize = stats.size;\n                \n                let newPosition;\n                switch (whence) {\n                    case 0: // SEEK_SET\n                        newPosition = offset;\n                        break;\n                    case 1: // SEEK_CUR\n                        newPosition = fileInfo.position + offset;\n                        break;\n                    case 2: // SEEK_END\n                        newPosition = fileSize + offset;\n                        break;\n                    default:\n                        this.registers[2] = -22; // Success\n    }\n    \n    sys_rename() {\n        const oldAddr = this.registers[4]; // a0\n        const newAddr = this.registers[5]; // a1\n        \n        // Read pathnames\n        let oldpath = '';\n        for (let i = oldAddr;            // X pixels per meter\n        buffer.writeUInt32LE(0, 42);            // Y pixels per meter\n        buffer.writeUInt32LE(0, 46);            // Colors in color table\n        buffer.writeUInt32LE(0, 50);            // Important color count\n        \n        // Read pixel data from screen buffer\n        const screenBufferOffset = this.screenBufferAddress;\n        let bufferOffset = headerSize;\n        \n        // Read in reverse order for BMP (bottom-up)\n        for (let y = height - 1; y >= 0; y--) {\n            for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {\n                const pixelAddr = screenBufferOffset + (y * width + x) * 4;\n                const pixelValue = this.loadWord(pixelAddr);\n                \n                // Extract RGBA components\n                const r = (pixelValue >> 0) & 0xFF;\n                const g = (pixelValue >> 8) & 0xFF;\n                const b = (pixelValue >> 16) & 0xFF;\n                const a = (pixelValue >> 24) & 0xFF;\n                \n                // Write as BGRA for BMP\n                buffer.writeUInt8(b, bufferOffset++);\n                buffer.writeUInt8(g, bufferOffset++);\n                buffer.writeUInt8(r, bufferOffset++);\n                buffer.writeUInt8(a, bufferOffset++);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Write to file\n        const filename = `/tmp/frame_${this.frameCount.toString().padStart(3, '0')}.bmp`;\n        fs.writeFileSync(filename, buffer);\n        console.log(`Saved frame ${this.frameCount} to ${filename}`);\n        \n        this.frameCount++;\n    }\n}\n\n// Main execution\nfunction main() {\n    const binaryPath = '/app/doomgeneric_mips';\n    \n    if (!fs.existsSync(binaryPath)) {\n        console.error(`MIPS binary not found: ${binaryPath}`);\n        process.exit(1);\n    }\n    \n    console.log('MIPS DOOM Interpreter');\n    console.log('====================');\n    \n    try {\n        const interpreter = new MIPSInterpreter(binaryPath);\n        interpreter.loadBinary();\n        interpreter.run();\n    } catch (e) {\n        console.error('Error running interpreter:', e);\n        process.exit(1);\n    }\n}\n\nmain();","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":839,"totalLines":839,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/vm.js","time":{"start":1789614106036,"end":1789614106139}},"id":"prt_0ad5024f2001f9hKcdXC3pAr9P","sessionID":"ses_f52b2c1a0ffeS9tBGuamY0bB6w","messageID":"msg_0ad500d0a001SFEonPqfdlPVoN"}}
